HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_198f24116164894bd50c","text":"3. **Spatial order replaces social broadcast.** **Lexical:** `س م ع` B005, \"reputation and public circulation,\" and `ل غ و` B004, a group's habitual speech-pattern. **Cue:** after public circulation is negated, the sequence communicates through a flowing source, raised couches, placed cups, rowed cushions, and spread carpets in 88:12-16. **Role:** the dormant circulation branch names what does not spread verbally; arranged objects carry the habitat's shared order instead. **Abduction:** I infer that material arrangement communicates welcome and status without speech. **Reading change:** the ayah can be read as a social design principle: belonging is made public by provision and placement, not by reputational chatter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_23c68dfca7e882778b33","text":"88:12-16 showed that negated speech does not produce an empty habitat. Flow, support, drink, rows, and spread textiles communicate order nonverbally, which was the surprising trigger for `س م ع` B005 and `ل غ و` B004 as a contrast between reputational circulation and materially displayed belonging.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B001","root_001361/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_29edc17b21eee1c05a61","text":"1. **The habitat removes both foul content and disruptive acoustic force.** **Lexical:** `س م ع` B001, hearing sounds with the ear; `ل غ و` B002, vain, false, or foul speech; and B003, noisy raised sound. **Cue:** categorical negation precedes an indefinite object, while the surrounding `فِيهَا` sequence replaces voices with a spring and visually ordered furnishings in 88:12-16. **Role:** hearing defines the sensory channel; B002 blocks corrupt semantic content and B003 blocks clamor as a material disturbance. **Abduction:** I infer total acoustic exclusion from negation plus indefiniteness. **Reading change:** the line promises not merely polite conversation but an environment free of both verbal injury and noise pollution.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2c5a504048ce2fcdda5a","text":"3. **No insulting exposure after faces have been exposed.** Evidence: `س م ع` B006 making someone hear abuse; `و ج ه` B001 face/front; `خ ش ع` B001 humiliation earlier. Structural cue: the humbled faces endure exposure; the easy faces enter a place without abusive hearing. Role: the absence of `لاغية` protects the face from social degradation. Abductive move: honor in the garden includes freedom from verbal assault. Reading change: the ayah becomes social and affective, not only linguistic.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B001","root_001361/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_44c53c2632aa1423ae19","text":"1. **Garden as protected soundscape.** Evidence: `س م ع` B001 hearing, B003 understanding/compliance; `ل غ و` B001 discarded non-counting thing, B002 vain/foul speech. Structural cue: after entry into the garden, the first furnishing is auditory absence. Role: hearing becomes a filter; `لاغية` is speech that does not count and is not allowed to define the place. Abductive move: blessed space is ordered by what cannot circulate in it. Reading change: the ayah reads as acoustic purification, not mere silence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_45fdfca16f5ba0970a2f","text":"2. **Nothing cognitively or morally nonproductive demands compliance there.** **Lexical:** `س م ع` B003, \"understanding and compliance,\" and `ل غ و` B001, \"the thing set aside as not counting.\" **Cue:** the ayah follows satisfaction with endeavor in 88:9 and a garden in 88:10, against the failed food economy of 88:6-7. **Role:** the hearing branch concerns speech that can call for acceptance or action; the non-counting branch identifies inputs that yield no valid consequence. **Abduction:** I infer an economy of attention parallel to the economy of provision: useless speech would consume response without producing value. **Reading change:** acoustic peace is also freedom from demands that neither count nor deserve action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B003","root_001361/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_483556936c2088646100","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:11:2\",\"root_000741\",[\"B003\"]],[\"88:11:4\",\"root_001361\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İşitmenin anlama ve uymayı da gerektirebilmesi, dikkati tükettiği halde hiçbir geçerli sonuç üretmeyen sözlere cevap verme yükünü ortadan kaldırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B003","root_000516/B009","root_001361/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_778ba03905e81196078b","text":"2. **The true report replaces vain noise.** Evidence: `ح د ث` B003 report in 88:1; `ل غ و` B002 false/foul speech, B003 noisy clamor; later `ذ ك ر` B009 reminder. Structural cue: the surah begins with `حديث`, removes `لاغية`, and commands reminder. Role: `لا تسمع` establishes the garden as the place where only meaningful report/reminder survives. Abductive move: speech is judged like deeds: some counts, some is thrown away. Reading change: the ayah reads as a speech-accounting scene.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7e9796be330c3b93e59b","text":"The final reminder command made this ayah define the difference between valid admonition and invalid noise. The account ending made `لاغية` literally non-counting speech: it has no weight in the ordered garden.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B001","root_001361/B002","root_001361/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c668ba3e125944c16c4a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:11:2\",\"root_000741\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:11:4\",\"root_001361\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bahçedeki işitsel arınma hem kötü ve boş içeriği hem de gürültünün maddi rahatsızlığını kaldırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B005","root_001361/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ce68acf3bbc0a0cbb53f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:11:2\",\"root_000741\",[\"B005\"]],[\"88:11:4\",\"root_001361\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İtibarın yayılması ve bir topluluğun alışılmış konuşması burada susturulurken, sonraki düzenli eşyalar aidiyet ve cömertliği sözsüz biçimde ilan eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000741/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_df231b615aa6118dc165","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:11:2\",\"root_000741\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Birine hakaret işittirmenin dışlanması, önce alçaltılmış yüzlerin karşısında rahat yüzleri toplumsal küçük düşürülmeden de korur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
